  45: // â”€â”€ Auth â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  46: 
  47: const TruecallerVerifyRequestSchema = z.object({
  48:   payload: z.string().min(1).openapi({ example: 'base64encodedPayload==' }),
  49:   signature: z.string().min(1).openapi({ example: 'base64encodedSignature==' }),
  50:   signatureAlgorithm: z.string().min(1).openapi({ example: 'SHA512withRSA' }),
  51:   fcmToken: z.string().optional().openapi({ example: 'fcm-token-abc123' }),
  52: }).openapi('TruecallerVerifyRequest');
  53: 
  54: const TruecallerVerifyResponseSchema = z.object({
  55:   firebaseCustomToken: z.string().openapi({ example: 'eyJhbGci...' }),
  56:   sessionExpiresAt: z.number().openapi({ example: 1700000000000 }),
  57: }).openapi('TruecallerVerifyResponse');
  58: 
  59: registry.register('TruecallerVerifyRequest', TruecallerVerifyRequestSchema);
  60: registry.register('TruecallerVerifyResponse', TruecallerVerifyResponseSchema);
  61: 
  62: registry.registerPath({
  63:   method: 'post',
  64:   path: '/v1/auth/truecaller/verify',
  65:   operationId: 'verifyTruecaller',
  66:   tags: ['auth'],
  67:   summary: 'Verify Truecaller profile signature and mint Firebase custom token',
  68:   description:
  69:     'Verifies the Truecaller SDK RSA payload/signature against the Truecaller public key API ' +
  70:     '(cached 24h in Cosmos). On success, mints a Firebase custom token for the verified phone number. ' +
  71:     'Called by customer-app when truecaller_server_verify_v2 flag is ON.',
  72:   request: {
  73:     body: {
  74:       content: { 'application/json': { schema: TruecallerVerifyRequestSchema } },
  75:     },
  76:   },
  77:   responses: {
  78:     200: {
  79:       description: 'Signature valid â€” Firebase custom token issued',
  80:       content: { 'application/json': { schema: TruecallerVerifyResponseSchema } },
  81:     },
  82:     400: {
  83:       description: 'Validation error or invalid signature',
  84:     },
  85:   },
  86: });
  87: 
  88: const HealthResponse = HealthResponseSchema.openapi('HealthResponse');
  89: registry.register('HealthResponse', HealthResponse);
  90: 
  91: registry.registerPath({
  92:   method: 'get',
  93:   path: '/v1/health',
  94:   operationId: 'getHealth',
  95:   tags: ['system'],
  96:   summary: 'Liveness probe',
  97:   description:
  98:     'Returns api/ liveness status plus build metadata. Unauthenticated. Never touches the database.',
  99:   responses: {
 100:     200: {
 101:       description: 'Service is live',
 102:       content: {
 103:         'application/json': { schema: HealthResponse },
 104:       },
 105:     },
 106:   },
 107: });

 311: const AdminComplaintDoc = ComplaintDocSchema.openapi('AdminComplaintDoc');
 312: registry.register('AdminComplaintDoc', AdminComplaintDoc);
 313: registry.register('CreateComplaintBody', CreateComplaintBodySchema);
 314: registry.register('PatchComplaintBody', PatchComplaintBodySchema);

 316: registry.register('RepeatOffendersResponse', RepeatOffendersResponseSchema);
 317: 
 318: registry.registerPath({
 319:   method: 'get', path: '/v1/admin/complaints', operationId: 'adminListComplaints',
 320:   tags: ['complaints'], summary: 'List complaints with optional filters',
 321:   security: [{ cookieAuth: [] }],
 322:   parameters: [
 323:     { name: 'status', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'string' } },
 324:     { name: 'assigneeAdminId', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'string' } },
 325:     { name: 'dateFrom', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'string' } },
 326:     { name: 'dateTo', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'string' } },
 327:     { name: 'resolvedSince', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'string' } },
 328:     { name: 'sortDir', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'string', enum: ['asc', 'desc'] } },
 329:     { name: 'page', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'integer', default: 1 } },
 330:     { name: 'pageSize', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'integer', default: 50 } },
 331:   ],
 332:   responses: {
 333:     200: { description: 'Paginated complaints list', content: { 'application/json': { schema: ComplaintListResponseSchema } } },
 334:     401: { description: 'Unauthenticated' },
 335:     403: { description: 'Forbidden' },
 336:   },
 337: });

 378: // Admin users
 379: const AdminRoleSchema = z.enum(['super-admin', 'ops-manager', 'finance', 'support-agent']).openapi('AdminRole');
 380: const AdminUserListItemSchema = z.object({
 381:   adminId: z.string(),
 382:   email: z.string().email(),
 383:   role: AdminRoleSchema,
 384:   displayName: z.string().optional(),
 385:   totpEnrolled: z.boolean(),
 386:   createdAt: z.string(),
 387:   updatedAt: z.string(),
 388:   deactivatedAt: z.string().nullable(),
 389: }).openapi('AdminUserListItem');
 390: const PatchAdminUserBodySchema = z.object({
 391:   role: AdminRoleSchema.optional(),
 392:   displayName: z.string().min(1).max(100).optional(),
 393:   deactivatedAt: z.string().nullable().optional(),
 394: }).strict().openapi('PatchAdminUserBody');
 395: 
 396: registry.register('AdminRole', AdminRoleSchema);
 397: registry.register('AdminUserListItem', AdminUserListItemSchema);
 398: registry.register('PatchAdminUserBody', PatchAdminUserBodySchema);
 399: 
 400: registry.registerPath({
 401:   method: 'get', path: '/v1/admin/users', operationId: 'adminListUsers',
 402:   tags: ['admin-users'], summary: 'List admin users without secrets',
 403:   security: [{ cookieAuth: [] }],
 404:   responses: {
 405:     200: { description: 'Admin users', content: { 'application/json': { schema: z.object({ users: z.array(AdminUserListItemSchema) }) } } },
 406:     401: { description: 'Unauthenticated' },

 408:   },
 409: });

 414:   security: [{ cookieAuth: [] }],
 415:   parameters: [{ name: 'adminId', in: 'path', required: true, schema: { type: 'string' } }],
 416:   request: { body: { content: { 'application/json': { schema: PatchAdminUserBodySchema } } } },
 417:   responses: {

 419:     400: { description: 'Validation error' },

 422:     404: { description: 'Admin user not found' },

 427: const ErasureRequestDoc = ErasureRequestDocSchema.openapi('ErasureRequestDoc');
 428: const AdminErasurePatchBody = AdminErasurePatchBodySchema.openapi('AdminErasurePatchBody');
 429: const SscLevyDoc = SscLevyDocSchema.openapi('SscLevyDoc');

 446:   parameters: [
 447:     { name: 'status', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'string' } },
 448:     { name: 'pageSize', in: 'query', required: false, schema: { type: 'integer', default: 50 } },

 452:     401: { description: 'Unauthenticated' },

 454:   },

 457: registry.registerPath({
 458:   method: 'patch', path: '/v1/admin/erasure-requests/{id}', operationId: 'adminPatchErasureRequest',
 459:   tags: ['compliance'], summary: 'Execute or deny an erasure request',

  15: import type { BookingDoc } from '../schemas/booking.js';
  16: import type { RatingDoc } from '../schemas/rating.js';

  18: import type { WalletLedgerEntry } from '../schemas/wallet-ledger.js';

  24: 
  25: const COMPLAINTS_CONTAINER = 'complaints';
  26: const TECHNICIANS_CONTAINER = 'technicians';
  27: const AUDIT_LOG_CONTAINER = 'audit_log';
  28: 
  29: export interface TechnicianFullExportDoc {
  30:   profile: TechnicianProfile | null;
  31:   kyc: TechnicianKyc | null;
  32:   fcmToken: string | null;
  33: }
  34: 
  35: async function listBookingsByCustomer(uid: string): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
  36:   const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
  37:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  38:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.customerId = @uid',
  39:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  40:     })
  41:     .fetchAll();
  42:   return resources.map((r) => BookingDocSchema.parse(r));
  43: }
  44: 
  45: async function listBookingsByTechnician(uid: string): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
  46:   const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
  47:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  48:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid',
  49:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  50:     })
  51:     .fetchAll();
  52:   return resources.map((r) => BookingDocSchema.parse(r));
  53: }
  54: 
  55: async function listRatingsByCustomer(uid: string): Promise<RatingDoc[]> {
  56:   const { resources } = await getRatingsContainer()
  57:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  58:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.customerId = @uid',
  59:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  60:     })
  61:     .fetchAll();
  62:   return resources.map((r) => RatingDocSchema.parse(r));
  63: }
  64: 
  65: async function listRatingsByTechnician(uid: string): Promise<RatingDoc[]> {
  66:   const { resources } = await getRatingsContainer()
  67:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  68:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid',
  69:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  70:     })
  71:     .fetchAll();
  72:   return resources.map((r) => RatingDocSchema.parse(r));
  73: }
  74: 
  75: export const userDataExportReads = {
  76:   async listBookingsForUser(uid: string, role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN'): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
  77:     return role === 'CUSTOMER' ? listBookingsByCustomer(uid) : listBookingsByTechnician(uid);
  78:   },
  79: 
  80:   async listRatingsForUser(uid: string, role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN'): Promise<RatingDoc[]> {
  81:     return role === 'CUSTOMER' ? listRatingsByCustomer(uid) : listRatingsByTechnician(uid);
  82:   },
  83: 
  84:   async listComplaintsForUser(uid: string): Promise<ComplaintDoc[]> {
  85:     const { resources } = await getCosmosClient()
  86:       .database(DB_NAME)
  87:       .container(COMPLAINTS_CONTAINER)
  88:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  89:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.customerId = @uid OR c.technicianId = @uid',
  90:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  91:       })
  92:       .fetchAll();
  93:     return resources.map((r) => ComplaintDocSchema.parse(r));
  94:   },
  95: 
  96:   async listWalletLedgerForTechnician(uid: string): Promise<WalletLedgerEntry[]> {
  97:     const { resources } = await getWalletLedgerContainer()
  98:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  99:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid',
 100:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
 101:       })
 102:       .fetchAll();
 103:     return resources.map((r) => WalletLedgerEntrySchema.parse(r));
 104:   },
 105: 
 106:   async listBookingEventsForUser(uid: string): Promise<BookingEventDoc[]> {
 107:     const { resources } = await getBookingEventsContainer()
 108:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
 109:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid OR c.adminId = @uid',
 110:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
 111:       })
 112:       .fetchAll();
 113:     return resources.map((r) => BookingEventDocSchema.parse(r));
 114:   },
 115: 
 116:   async listDispatchAttemptsForUser(uid: string): Promise<DispatchAttemptDoc[]> {
 117:     const { resources } = await getDispatchAttemptsContainer()
 118:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
 119:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE ARRAY_CONTAINS(c.technicianIds, @uid)',
 120:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
 121:       })
 122:       .fetchAll();
 123:     return resources.map((r) => DispatchAttemptDocSchema.parse(r));
 124:   },
 125: 
 126:   async readTechnicianFullDoc(uid: string): Promise<TechnicianFullExportDoc> {
 127:     const { resource } = await getCosmosClient()
 128:       .database(DB_NAME)
 129:       .container(TECHNICIANS_CONTAINER)
 130:       .item(uid, uid)
 131:       .read<Record<string, unknown>>();
 132:     if (!resource) return { profile: null, kyc: null, fcmToken: null };
 133:     const r = resource as {
 134:       kyc?: TechnicianKyc;
 135:       fcmToken?: string;
 136:     } & Record<string, unknown>;
 137:     const { kyc, fcmToken, ...rest } = r;
 138:     return {
 139:       profile: rest as unknown as TechnicianProfile,
 140:       kyc: kyc ?? null,
 141:       fcmToken: fcmToken ?? null,
 142:     };
 143:   },
 144: 
 145:   async listAuditLogForUser(uid: string, sinceIso: string): Promise<AuditLogEntry[]> {
 146:     const { resources } = await getCosmosClient()
 147:       .database(DB_NAME)
 148:       .container(AUDIT_LOG_CONTAINER)
 149:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
 150:         query:
 151:           'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.resourceId = @uid AND c.timestamp >= @since ORDER BY c.timestamp DESC',
 152:         parameters: [
 153:           { name: '@uid', value: uid },
 154:           { name: '@since', value: sinceIso },
 155:         ],
 156:       })
 157:       .fetchAll();
 158:     return resources.map((r) => AuditLogEntrySchema.parse(r));
 159:   },
 160: };

diff --git a/api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts b/api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..79d9a380
--- /dev/null
+++ b/api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,178 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env tsx
+/**
+ * E19-S01 migration: backfill panMaskedNumber + panHash for all technician docs
+ * that still have a legacy panNumber or panNumberEncrypted field.
+ *
+ * Usage:
+ *   pnpm tsx api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts --dry-run   # preview only
+ *   pnpm tsx api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts             # live run
+ *
+ * Idempotent: docs that already have panHash and no panNumber are skipped.
+ * Safe to run multiple times.
+ *
+ * Prerequisites:
+ *   - COSMOS_CONNECTION_STRING (or endpoint+key) env vars set
+ *   - COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY set (needed to decrypt legacy panNumberEncrypted blobs)
+ *   - COSMOS_DATABASE set
+ */
+
+import { getCosmosClient, DB_NAME } from '../src/cosmos/client.js';
+import { decryptPan } from '../src/services/piiCrypto.service.js';
+import { maskPan, hashPan } from '../src/services/pan.utils.js';
+import type { EncryptedPan } from '../src/schemas/kyc.js';
+
+const DRY_RUN = process.argv.includes('--dry-run');
+
+interface KycSubdoc {
+  panNumber?: string | null;
+  panMaskedNumber?: string | null;
+  panHash?: string | null;
+  panNumberEncrypted?: EncryptedPan;
+  [key: string]: unknown;
+}
+
+interface TechnicianDoc {
+  id: string;
+  kyc?: KycSubdoc;
+  [key: string]: unknown;
+}
+
+function derivePanFields(kyc: KycSubdoc): {
+  panMaskedNumber: string | null;
+  panHash: string | null;
+  source: string;
+} {
+  // Strategy 1: decrypt the AES-GCM blob â†’ get raw PAN â†’ hash + mask
+  if (kyc.panNumberEncrypted) {
+    try {
+      const raw = decryptPan(kyc.panNumberEncrypted);
+      const masked = maskPan(raw);
+      if (masked) {
+        return {
+          panMaskedNumber: masked,
+          panHash: hashPan(raw),
+          source: 'decrypted',
+        };
+      }
+    } catch {
+      // Blob corrupt or key rotation â€” fall through to next strategy
+    }
+  }
+
+  // Strategy 2: panNumber contains a raw canonical PAN (pre-masking era docs)
+  if (kyc.panNumber) {
+    const masked = maskPan(kyc.panNumber);
+    if (masked) {
+      return {
+        panMaskedNumber: masked,
+        panHash: hashPan(kyc.panNumber),
+        source: 'raw-pan-number',
+      };
+    }
+
+    // Strategy 3: panNumber looks like a known-masked format â€” copy only if recognised.
+    // Only accept: old-style ABCDE####F  OR  new-style XXXXX1234F
+    // Anything else (OCR artifact, raw PAN with spaces, truncated) must NOT be copied
+    // verbatim because callers trust panMaskedNumber to be safe.
+    const KNOWN_MASKED = /^(?:[A-Z]{5}#{4}[A-Z]|XXXXX\d{4}[A-Z])$/;
+    if (KNOWN_MASKED.test(kyc.panNumber)) {
+      return {
+        panMaskedNumber: kyc.panNumber,
+        panHash: null,
+        source: 'pre-masked-no-hash',
+      };
+    }
+
+    // Unrecognised format â€” leave masked null; keep encrypted blob for future retry
+    return { panMaskedNumber: kyc.panMaskedNumber ?? null, panHash: null, source: 'unrecognized-format' };
+  }
+
+  // Preserve any mask written on a previous run so retries are idempotent
+  return { panMaskedNumber: kyc.panMaskedNumber ?? null, panHash: null, source: 'no-pan-data' };
+}
+
+async function run(): Promise<void> {
+  console.log(`\nE19-S01 PAN migration (${DRY_RUN ? 'DRY RUN â€” no writes' : 'LIVE RUN'})\n`);
+
+  const container = getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container('technicians');
+
+  const { resources: docs } = await container.items
+    .query<TechnicianDoc>('SELECT * FROM c')
+    .fetchAll();
+
+  console.log(`Total technician docs fetched: ${docs.length}\n`);
+
+  let skipped = 0;
+  let processed = 0;
+  let noAction = 0;
+  let errors = 0;
+
+  for (const doc of docs) {
+    const kyc = doc.kyc;
+
+    if (!kyc) {
+      skipped++;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    // Idempotent: already migrated
+    if (kyc.panHash && !kyc.panNumber) {
+      skipped++;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    // Nothing to migrate
+    if (!kyc.panNumber && !kyc.panNumberEncrypted) {
+      noAction++;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      const { panMaskedNumber, panHash, source } = derivePanFields(kyc);
+
+      console.log(
+        `[${doc.id}] source=${source} hasMasked=${panMaskedNumber !== null} hasHash=${panHash !== null}`,
+      );
+
+      if (!DRY_RUN) {
+        const updatedKyc: KycSubdoc = {
+          ...kyc,
+          panMaskedNumber,
+          panHash,
+          panNumber: null,
+        };
+        // Only remove the encrypted blob once a deterministic hash has been derived.
+        // If panHash is null the blob may still be decryptable on a future retry
+        // (e.g. after key rotation is corrected) â€” keep it to avoid irrecoverable loss.
+        if (panHash !== null) {
+          delete updatedKyc['panNumberEncrypted'];
+        }
+
+        await container.items.upsert({ ...doc, kyc: updatedKyc });
+      }
+
+      processed++;
+    } catch (err) {
+      console.error(`[${doc.id}] ERROR:`, err);
+      errors++;
+    }
+  }
+
+  console.log(
+    `\nSummary: processed=${processed} skipped(already-done)=${skipped} no-action=${noAction} errors=${errors}`,
+  );
+  if (DRY_RUN) {
+    console.log('DRY RUN complete â€” no Cosmos writes performed.');
+  } else {
+    console.log('Migration complete.');
+    if (errors > 0) {
+      console.warn(`âš  ${errors} doc(s) failed â€” re-run after investigating.`);
+      process.exitCode = 1;
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+run().catch((err: unknown) => {
+  console.error('Fatal:', err);
+  process.exit(1);
+});

@@ -35,7 +40,8 @@ export async function getKycStatus(

       aadhaarMaskedNumber: kyc.aadhaarMaskedNumber,
-      panNumber: kyc.panNumber,

+      panNumber: panMaskedValue, // legacy alias â€” technician-app KycStatusResponse reads panNumber (migration window)

diff --git a/api/src/services/pan.utils.ts b/api/src/services/pan.utils.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..261a8d68
--- /dev/null
+++ b/api/src/services/pan.utils.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+
+const PAN_REGEX = /^[A-Z]{5}\d{4}[A-Z]$/;
+
+export function normalizePan(pan: string): string {
+  return pan.trim().toUpperCase();
+}
+
+export function maskPan(pan: string): string | null {
+  const normalized = normalizePan(pan);
+  if (!PAN_REGEX.test(normalized)) return null;
+  return `XXXXX${normalized.slice(5)}`;
+}
+
+export function hashPan(pan: string): string {
+  return createHash('sha256').update(normalizePan(pan)).digest('hex');
+}
diff --git a/api/tests/cosmos/technician-repository.test.ts b/api/tests/cosmos/technician-repository.test.ts
index 7b5f36b2..96a3c229 100644
--- a/api/tests/cosmos/technician-repository.test.ts
+++ b/api/tests/cosmos/technician-repository.test.ts
@@ -46,6 +46,49 @@ describe('upsertKycStatus', () => {
       kyc: expect.objectContaining({ kycStatus: 'PENDING' }),
     }));
   });
+
+  it('[E19-S01] writes panMaskedNumber + panHash when provided, zeroes raw panNumber', async () => {
+    const mockUpsert = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({});
+    const mockRead = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ resource: { id: 'tech_1' } });
+    (getCosmosClient as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockReturnValue({
+      database: () => ({ container: () => ({
+        item: () => ({ read: mockRead }),
+        items: { upsert: mockUpsert },
+      }) }),
+    });
+
+    const fakeHash = 'a'.repeat(64);
+    await upsertKycStatus('tech_1', {
+      kycStatus: 'PAN_DONE',
+      panMaskedNumber: 'XXXXX1234F',
+      panHash: fakeHash,
+      panNumber: null,
+    });
+
+    const call = mockUpsert.mock.calls[0];
+    const kyc = (call?.[0] as Record<string, unknown>)['kyc'] as Record<string, unknown>;
+    expect(kyc['panMaskedNumber']).toBe('XXXXX1234F');
+    expect(kyc['panHash']).toBe(fakeHash);
+    expect(kyc['panNumber']).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('[E19-S01] initializes panMaskedNumber and panHash to null for new docs', async () => {
+    const mockUpsert = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({});
+    const mockRead = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ resource: undefined });
+    (getCosmosClient as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockReturnValue({
+      database: () => ({ container: () => ({
+        item: () => ({ read: mockRead }),
+        items: { upsert: mockUpsert },
+      }) }),
+    });
+
+    await upsertKycStatus('tech_fresh', { kycStatus: 'PENDING' });
+
+    const call = mockUpsert.mock.calls[0];
+    const kyc = (call?.[0] as Record<string, unknown>)['kyc'] as Record<string, unknown>;
+    expect(kyc['panMaskedNumber']).toBeNull();
+    expect(kyc['panHash']).toBeNull();
+  });
 });

+    vi.mocked(getKycByTechnicianId).mockResolvedValue({
+      aadhaarVerified: true,
+      aadhaarMaskedNumber: null,
+      panNumber: 'ABCDE1234F',  // pre-E18-S06 raw PAN â€” must be masked before returning
+      panImagePath: null,
+      kycStatus: 'PAN_DONE',
+      updatedAt: '2026-03-01T00:00:00Z',
+    });
+
+    const req = new HttpRequest({
+      method: 'GET',
+      url: 'http://localhost/v1/kyc/status?technicianId=tech-001',
+      headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer valid' },
+    });
+    const res = await handler(req, new InvocationContext());
+
+    const body = res.jsonBody as Record<string, unknown>;
+    expect(body['panMaskedNumber']).toBe('XXXXX1234F');
+    expect(body['panMaskedNumber']).not.toBe('ABCDE1234F');  // raw PAN must never appear
   });
 });

diff --git a/api/tests/services/pan.utils.test.ts b/api/tests/services/pan.utils.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..1d058689
--- /dev/null
+++ b/api/tests/services/pan.utils.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
+import { normalizePan, maskPan, hashPan } from '../../src/services/pan.utils.js';
+
+describe('normalizePan', () => {
+  it('trims leading/trailing whitespace and uppercases', () => {
+    expect(normalizePan('  abcde1234f  ')).toBe('ABCDE1234F');
+    expect(normalizePan('ABCDE1234F')).toBe('ABCDE1234F');
+    expect(normalizePan('abcde1234f')).toBe('ABCDE1234F');
+  });
+
+  it('does not strip interior whitespace', () => {
+    expect(normalizePan('ABCDE 1234F')).toBe('ABCDE 1234F');
+  });
+});
+
+describe('maskPan', () => {
+  it('masks first 5 chars with XXXXX for canonical 10-char PAN', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('ABCDE1234F')).toBe('XXXXX1234F');
+    expect(maskPan('XYZPQ9876R')).toBe('XXXXX9876R');
+  });
+
+  it('normalizes lowercase input before masking', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('abcde1234f')).toBe('XXXXX1234F');
+  });
+
+  it('strips leading/trailing whitespace before masking', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('  ABCDE1234F  ')).toBe('XXXXX1234F');
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for PAN with interior whitespace', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('ABCDE 1234F')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for too-short PAN', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('ABCDE1234')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for too-long PAN', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('ABCDE12345F')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for PAN starting with a digit', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('1BCDE1234F')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for empty string', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for PAN ending with a digit (check letter must be alpha)', () => {
+    expect(maskPan('ABCDE12345')).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
+
+describe('hashPan', () => {
+  it('returns a 64-char lowercase hex string (SHA-256)', () => {
+    const h = hashPan('ABCDE1234F');
+    expect(h).toHaveLength(64);
+    expect(h).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
+  });
+
+  it('is deterministic â€” same normalized input â†’ same hash', () => {
+    expect(hashPan('ABCDE1234F')).toBe(hashPan('ABCDE1234F'));
+  });
+
+  it('normalizes before hashing â€” lowercase and padded inputs yield same hash', () => {
+    expect(hashPan('ABCDE1234F')).toBe(hashPan('abcde1234f'));
+    expect(hashPan('ABCDE1234F')).toBe(hashPan('  ABCDE1234F  '));
+  });
+
+  it('produces different hashes for different PANs', () => {
+    expect(hashPan('ABCDE1234F')).not.toBe(hashPan('XYZPQ9876R'));
+  });
+
+  it('[DPDP] raw PAN cannot be retrieved from hash (one-way property)', () => {
+    const h = hashPan('ABCDE1234F');
+    // The hash must not contain the raw PAN as a substring
+    expect(h).not.toContain('ABCDE1234F');
+    expect(h).not.toContain('abcde1234f');
+  });
+});

 121:     id: technicianId,
 122:     technicianId: (resource?.technicianId as string | undefined) ?? technicianId,
 123:     ...(patch.isOnline !== undefined ? { isOnline: patch.isOnline } : {}),
 124:     ...(patch.isAvailable !== undefined ? { isAvailable: patch.isAvailable } : {}),
 125:     ...(patch.availabilityWindows !== undefined ? { availabilityWindows: patch.availabilityWindows } : {}),
 126:     updatedAt,
 127:   };
 128:   await container.items.upsert(updated);
 129:   return normalizeAvailability(updated);
 130: }
 131: 
 132: async function readTechnicianDocument(technicianId: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown> | null> {
 133:   const container = getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 134:   try {
 135:     const { resource } = await container.item(technicianId, technicianId).read<Record<string, unknown>>();
 136:     return resource ?? null;
 137:   } catch (err: unknown) {
 138:     if ((err as { code?: number }).code === 404) return null;
 139:     throw err;
 140:   }
 141: }
 142: 
 143: function toServiceProfile(doc: Record<string, unknown> | null): TechnicianServiceProfile {
 144:   const skills = Array.isArray(doc?.skills) ? doc.skills.filter((skill): skill is string => typeof skill === 'string') : [];
 145:   const location = doc?.location as { coordinates?: unknown } | undefined;
 146:   const coordinates = location?.coordinates;
 147:   const hasCoordinates =
 148:     Array.isArray(coordinates) &&
 149:     coordinates.length >= 2 &&
 150:     typeof coordinates[0] === 'number' &&
 151:     typeof coordinates[1] === 'number';
 152:   return {
 153:     skills,
 154:     location: hasCoordinates ? { lat: coordinates[1] as number, lng: coordinates[0] as number } : null,
 155:   };
 156: }
 157: 
 158: export async function getTechnicianServiceProfile(
 159:   technicianId: string,
 160: ): Promise<TechnicianServiceProfile> {
 161:   return toServiceProfile(await readTechnicianDocument(technicianId));
 162: }
 163: 
 164: export async function patchTechnicianServiceProfile(
 165:   technicianId: string,
 166:   patch: TechnicianServiceProfilePatch,
 167: ): Promise<TechnicianServiceProfile> {
 168:   const container = getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 169:   const existing = await readTechnicianDocument(technicianId);
 170:   const existingKyc = existing?.kyc as { kycStatus?: unknown } | undefined;
 171:   const updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
 172:   const updated: Record<string, unknown> = {
 173:     ...(existing ?? {}),
 174:     id: technicianId,
 175:     technicianId: typeof existing?.technicianId === 'string' ? existing.technicianId : technicianId,
 176:     skills: patch.skills,
 177:     availabilityWindows: Array.isArray(existing?.availabilityWindows) ? existing.availabilityWindows : [],
 178:     isOnline: typeof existing?.isOnline === 'boolean' ? existing.isOnline : false,
 179:     isAvailable: typeof existing?.isAvailable === 'boolean' ? existing.isAvailable : false,
 180:     kycStatus: typeof existing?.kycStatus === 'string'
 181:       ? existing.kycStatus
 182:       : typeof existingKyc?.kycStatus === 'string'
 183:         ? existingKyc.kycStatus
 184:         : 'PENDING',
 185:     updatedAt,
 186:   };
 187:   if (patch.location !== undefined) {
 188:     updated.location = { type: 'Point', coordinates: [patch.location.lng, patch.location.lat] };
 189:   }
 190:   await container.items.upsert(updated);
 191:   return toServiceProfile(updated);
 192: }
 193: 
 194: export async function getTechniciansWithinRadius(
 195:   lat: number,
 196:   lng: number,
 197:   radiusKm: number,
 198:   serviceId: string,
 199: ): Promise<TechnicianProfile[]> {
 200:   const client = getCosmosClient();
 201:   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 202:   const polygon = boundingBoxPolygon(lat, lng, radiusKm);
 203:   const query = {
 204:     query: `SELECT * FROM c
 205:             WHERE ST_WITHIN(c.location, @polygon)
 206:             AND ARRAY_CONTAINS(c.skills, @serviceId)
 207:             AND c.isOnline = true
 208:             AND c.isAvailable = true`,
 209:     parameters: [
 210:       { name: '@polygon', value: polygon as unknown as string },
 211:       { name: '@serviceId', value: serviceId },
 212:     ],
 213:   };
 214:   const { resources } = await container.items
 215:     .query<TechnicianProfile>(query)
 216:     .fetchAll();
 217:   return resources;
 218: }

 230: export async function getTechniciansByIds(
 231:   technicianIds: string[],
 232: ): Promise<TechnicianLookupInfo[]> {
 233:   const ids = [...new Set(technicianIds.filter(Boolean))];
 234:   if (ids.length === 0) return [];
 235: 
 236:   const client = getCosmosClient();
 237:   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 238:   const { resources } = await container.items
 239:     .query<TechnicianLookupInfo>({
 240:       query: `SELECT c.id, c.technicianId, c.displayName, c.name, c.rating, c.isOnline, c.isAvailable
 241:               FROM c
 242:               WHERE ARRAY_CONTAINS(@ids, c.id) OR ARRAY_CONTAINS(@ids, c.technicianId)`,
 243:       parameters: [{ name: '@ids', value: ids }],
 244:     })
 245:     .fetchAll();
 246:   return resources;
 247: }

 249: function technicianDisplayName(tech: TechnicianProfile & Record<string, unknown>): string {
 250:   const displayName = tech['displayName'];
 251:   if (typeof displayName === 'string' && displayName.trim() !== '') return displayName;
 252:   const name = tech['name'];
 253:   if (typeof name === 'string' && name.trim() !== '') return name;
 254:   return tech.technicianId || tech.id;
 255: }
 256: 
 257: export interface TechnicianCandidate {
 258:   technicianId: string;
 259:   displayName: string;
 260:   distanceKm: number;
 261:   rating?: number;
 262:   isOnline: boolean;
 263:   isAvailable: boolean;
 264: }
 265: 
 266: export async function getTechnicianCandidatesForBooking(
 267:   booking: BookingDoc,
 268:   radiusKm: number,
 269: ): Promise<TechnicianCandidate[]> {
 270:   const { lat, lng } = booking.addressLatLng;
 271:   const assignedTechnicianIds = new Set(booking.technicianId ? [booking.technicianId] : []);
 272:   const candidates = await getTechniciansWithinRadius(lat, lng, radiusKm, booking.serviceId);
 273:   return candidates
 274:     .map((tech) => {
 275:       const distanceKm = haversine(lat, lng, tech.location.coordinates[1], tech.location.coordinates[0]);
 276:       return { tech: tech, distanceKm };
 277:     })
 278:     .filter(({ tech, distanceKm }) =>
 279:       distanceKm <= radiusKm &&
 280:       !assignedTechnicianIds.has(tech.id) &&
 281:       !assignedTechnicianIds.has(tech.technicianId)
 282:     )
 283:     .filter(({ tech }) => !(tech.blockedCustomerIds ?? []).includes(booking.customerId))
 284:     .sort((a, b) => {
 285:       if (a.distanceKm !== b.distanceKm) return a.distanceKm - b.distanceKm;
 286:       return (b.tech.rating ?? 0) - (a.tech.rating ?? 0);
 287:     })
 288:     .map(({ tech, distanceKm }) => {
 289:       const candidate: TechnicianCandidate = {
 290:         technicianId: tech.technicianId || tech.id,
 291:         displayName: technicianDisplayName(tech),
 292:         distanceKm: Number(distanceKm.toFixed(2)),
 293:         isOnline: tech.isOnline,
 294:         isAvailable: tech.isAvailable,
 295:       };
 296:       if (tech.rating !== undefined) candidate.rating = tech.rating;
 297:       return candidate;
 298:     });
 299: }

 301: // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Settlement helpers (E06-S04) Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬

 322: export async function incrementCompletedJobCount(technicianId: string): Promise<void> {
 323:   const container = getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 324:   const maxRetries = 3;
 325:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
 326:     const { resource, etag } = await container
 327:       .item(technicianId, technicianId)
 328:       .read<{ id: string; completedJobCount?: number } & Record<string, unknown>>();
 329:     if (!resource) return;
 330:     try {
 331:       await container.item(technicianId, technicianId).replace(
 332:         { ...resource, completedJobCount: (resource.completedJobCount ?? 0) + 1 },
 333:         { accessCondition: { type: 'IfMatch', condition: etag ?? '' } },
 334:       );
 335:       return;
 336:     } catch (err: unknown) {
 337:       if ((err as { code?: number }).code === 412 && attempt < maxRetries - 1) {
 338:         continue;
 339:       }
 340:       throw err;
 341:     }
 342:   }
 343: }

 345: // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Payout cadence helpers (E08-S02) Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬

 347: export async function updatePayoutCadence(
 348:   technicianId: string,
 349:   cadence: 'WEEKLY' | 'NEXT_DAY' | 'INSTANT',
 350: ): Promise<void> {
 351:   const container = getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 352:   const maxRetries = 3;

 354:     const { resource, etag } = await container
 355:       .item(technicianId, technicianId)
 356:       .read<{ id: string; payoutCadence?: string; payoutCadenceUpdatedAt?: string } & Record<string, unknown>>();
 357:     if (!resource) return;
 358:     try {
 359:       await container.item(technicianId, technicianId).replace(
 360:         { ...resource, payoutCadence: cadence, payoutCadenceUpdatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
 361:         { accessCondition: { type: 'IfMatch', condition: etag ?? '' } },
 362:       );
 363:       return;
 364:     } catch (err: unknown) {
 365:       if ((err as { code?: number }).code === 412 && attempt < maxRetries - 1) {
 366:         continue;
 367:       }
 368:       throw err;
 369:     }
 370:   }
 371: }

 384: // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Shield helpers (E08-S04) Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬

 386: export async function addBlockedCustomer(
 387:   technicianId: string,
 388:   customerId: string,
 389: ): Promise<void> {
 390:   const container = getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 391:   const maxRetries = 3;
 392:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
 393:     const { resource, etag } = await container
 394:       .item(technicianId, technicianId)
 395:       .read<{ id: string; blockedCustomerIds?: string[] } & Record<string, unknown>>();
 396:     if (!resource) return;
 397:     if (resource.blockedCustomerIds?.includes(customerId)) return; // idempotent
 398:     const updated = {
 399:       ...resource,
 400:       blockedCustomerIds: [...(resource.blockedCustomerIds ?? []), customerId],
 401:     };
 402:     try {
 403:       await container.item(technicianId, technicianId).replace(updated, {
 404:         accessCondition: { type: 'IfMatch', condition: etag ?? '' },
 405:       });
 406:       return;
 407:     } catch (err: unknown) {
 408:       if ((err as { code?: number }).code === 412 && attempt < maxRetries - 1) continue;
 409:       throw err;
 410:     }
 411:   }
 412: }

--- api/src/functions/kyc/get-kyc-status.ts
   1: import { app, HttpRequest, HttpResponseInit, InvocationContext } from '@azure/functions';
   2: import { verifyTechnicianToken } from '../../middleware/verifyTechnicianToken.js';
   3: import { getKycByTechnicianId } from '../../cosmos/technician-repository.js';
   4: import { maskPan } from '../../services/pan.utils.js';
   5: 
   6: export async function getKycStatus(
   7:   req: HttpRequest,
   8:   _ctx: InvocationContext
   9: ): Promise<HttpResponseInit> {
  10:   let decodedToken: { uid: string };
  11:   try {
  12:     decodedToken = await verifyTechnicianToken(req);
  13:   } catch {
  14:     return { status: 401, jsonBody: { error: 'Unauthorized' } };
  15:   }
  16: 
  17:   const technicianId = req.query.get('technicianId');
  18:   if (!technicianId) {
  19:     return { status: 400, jsonBody: { error: 'technicianId query param required' } };
  20:   }
  21: 
  22:   // P1-B: caller may only read their own KYC record
  23:   if (decodedToken.uid !== technicianId) {
  24:     return { status: 403, jsonBody: { error: 'Forbidden' } };
  25:   }
  26: 
  27:   const kyc = await getKycByTechnicianId(technicianId);
  28:   if (!kyc) {
  29:     return { status: 404, jsonBody: { error: 'KYC record not found' } };
  30:   }
  31: 
  32:   // migration window: prefer new panMaskedNumber; fall back to masking legacy panNumber
  33:   // maskPan handles raw canonical PANs; ?? kyc.panNumber handles already-masked legacy values
  34:   const panMaskedValue = kyc.panMaskedNumber ?? (kyc.panNumber ? maskPan(kyc.panNumber) ?? kyc.panNumber : null);
  35: 
  36:   return {
  37:     status: 200,
  38:     jsonBody: {
  39:       technicianId,
  40:       kycStatus: kyc.kycStatus,
  41:       aadhaarVerified: kyc.aadhaarVerified,
  42:       aadhaarMaskedNumber: kyc.aadhaarMaskedNumber,
  43:       panMaskedNumber: panMaskedValue,
  44:       panNumber: panMaskedValue, // legacy alias Ã¢â‚¬â€ technician-app KycStatusResponse reads panNumber (migration window)
  45:     },
  46:   };
  47: }
  48: 
  49: app.http('getKycStatus', {
  50:   methods: ['GET'],
  51:   authLevel: 'anonymous',
  52:   route: 'v1/kyc/status',
  53:   handler: getKycStatus,
  54: });

   1: import { app, HttpRequest, HttpResponseInit, InvocationContext } from '@azure/functions';
   2: import { extractPanFromStoragePath } from '../../services/formRecognizer.service.js';
   3: import { upsertKycStatus } from '../../cosmos/technician-repository.js';
   4: import { verifyTechnicianToken } from '../../middleware/verifyTechnicianToken.js';
   5: import { SubmitPanOcrRequestSchema } from '../../schemas/kyc.js';
   6: import { kycAuditEntry } from '../../services/kycAudit.service.js';
   7: 
   8: export async function submitPanOcr(
   9:   req: HttpRequest,
  10:   _ctx: InvocationContext
  11: ): Promise<HttpResponseInit> {
  12:   let decodedToken: { uid: string };
  13:   try {
  14:     decodedToken = await verifyTechnicianToken(req);
  15:   } catch {
  16:     return { status: 401, jsonBody: { error: 'Unauthorized' } };
  17:   }
  18: 
  19:   let body: unknown;
  20:   try {
  21:     body = await req.json();
  22:   } catch {
  23:     return { status: 400, jsonBody: { error: 'Invalid JSON' } };
  24:   }
  25: 
  26:   const parsed = SubmitPanOcrRequestSchema.safeParse(body);
  27:   if (!parsed.success) {
  28:     return { status: 422, jsonBody: { error: parsed.error.flatten() } };
  29:   }
  30: 
  31:   const { technicianId, firebaseStoragePath } = parsed.data;
  32: 
  33:   // P1-C: caller may only update their own KYC record
  34:   if (decodedToken.uid !== technicianId) {
  35:     return { status: 403, jsonBody: { error: 'Forbidden' } };
  36:   }
  37: 
  38:   const ocrResult = await extractPanFromStoragePath(firebaseStoragePath);
  39: 
  40:   if (ocrResult.status === 'PAN_DONE') {
  41:     // Raw PAN discarded inside formRecognizer.service; only hash+mask reach here
  42:     await upsertKycStatus(technicianId, {
  43:       panMaskedNumber: ocrResult.panMaskedNumber,
  44:       panHash: ocrResult.panHash,
  45:       panNumber: null,           // explicitly clear legacy field
  46:       panNumberEncrypted: undefined, // explicitly clear
  47:       panImagePath: firebaseStoragePath,
  48:       kycStatus: 'PAN_DONE',
  49:     });
  50:     void kycAuditEntry(technicianId, 'PAN', 'VERIFIED');
  51:     return {
  52:       status: 200,
  53:       jsonBody: {
  54:         kycStatus: 'PAN_DONE',
  55:         panMaskedNumber: ocrResult.panMaskedNumber,
  56:         panNumber: ocrResult.panMaskedNumber, // legacy alias Ã¢â‚¬â€ technician-app reads panNumber (migration window)
  57:       },
  58:     };
  59:   }
  60: 
  61:   // Explicitly clear any stale PAN fields Ã¢â‚¬â€ a rejected submission must not leave
  62:   // a previous panMaskedNumber visible via getKycStatus while status is MANUAL_REVIEW.
  63:   await upsertKycStatus(technicianId, {
  64:     panMaskedNumber: null,
  65:     panHash: null,
  66:     panNumber: null,
  67:     panNumberEncrypted: undefined,
  68:     panImagePath: firebaseStoragePath,
  69:     kycStatus: 'MANUAL_REVIEW',
  70:   });
  71:   void kycAuditEntry(technicianId, 'PAN', 'REJECTED');
  72:   return { status: 200, jsonBody: { kycStatus: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null } };
  73: }
  74: 
  75: app.http('submitPanOcr', {
  76:   methods: ['POST'],
  77:   authLevel: 'anonymous',
  78:   route: 'v1/kyc/pan-ocr',
  79:   handler: submitPanOcr,
  80: });
--- api/src/schemas/kyc.ts
   1: import { z } from 'zod';
   2: import { extendZodWithOpenApi } from '@asteasolutions/zod-to-openapi';
   3: 
   4: extendZodWithOpenApi(z);
   5: 
   6: export const KycStatusSchema = z.enum([
   7:   'PENDING', 'AADHAAR_DONE', 'PAN_DONE', 'COMPLETE', 'PENDING_MANUAL', 'MANUAL_REVIEW',
   8: ]);
   9: 
  10: export const EncryptedPanSchema = z.object({
  11:   iv: z.string(),
  12:   ciphertext: z.string(),
  13:   tag: z.string(),
  14:   v: z.literal(1),
  15: });
  16: 
  17: export const TechnicianKycSchema = z.object({
  18:   aadhaarVerified: z.boolean(),
  19:   aadhaarMaskedNumber: z.string().nullable(),
  20:   panNumber: z.string().nullable(),                   // legacy Ã¢â‚¬â€ migration reads only
  21:   panMaskedNumber: z.string().nullable().optional(),  // new (E19-S01): XXXXX1234F format
  22:   panHash: z.string().nullable().optional(),          // new (E19-S01): SHA-256 hex, 64 chars
  23:   panImagePath: z.string().nullable(),
  24:   panNumberEncrypted: EncryptedPanSchema.optional(),  // legacy Ã¢â‚¬â€ kept for migration decryption
  25:   kycStatus: KycStatusSchema,
  26:   updatedAt: z.string().datetime(),
  27: });
  28: 
  29: export const SubmitAadhaarRequestSchema = z.object({
  30:   technicianId: z.string().min(1),
  31:   authCode: z.string(),
  32:   redirectUri: z.string().url(),
  33: });
  34: 
  35: export const SubmitAadhaarResponseSchema = z.object({
  36:   kycStatus: KycStatusSchema,
  37:   aadhaarMaskedNumber: z.string().nullable(),
  38:   aadhaarVerified: z.boolean(),
  39: });
  40: 
  41: export const SubmitPanOcrRequestSchema = z.object({
  42:   technicianId: z.string().min(1),
  43:   firebaseStoragePath: z.string().min(1),
  44: });
  45: 
  46: export const SubmitPanOcrResponseSchema = z.object({
  47:   kycStatus: KycStatusSchema,
  48:   panMaskedNumber: z.string().nullable(),
  49: });
  50: 
  51: export const GetKycStatusResponseSchema = z.object({
  52:   technicianId: z.string(),
  53:   kycStatus: KycStatusSchema,
  54:   aadhaarVerified: z.boolean(),
  55:   aadhaarMaskedNumber: z.string().nullable(),
  56:   panMaskedNumber: z.string().nullable(),
  57: });
  58: 
  59: export type EncryptedPan = z.infer<typeof EncryptedPanSchema>;
  60: export type TechnicianKyc = z.infer<typeof TechnicianKycSchema>;
  61: export type KycStatus = z.infer<typeof KycStatusSchema>;
  62: export type SubmitAadhaarRequest = z.infer<typeof SubmitAadhaarRequestSchema>;
  63: export type SubmitAadhaarResponse = z.infer<typeof SubmitAadhaarResponseSchema>;
  64: export type SubmitPanOcrRequest = z.infer<typeof SubmitPanOcrRequestSchema>;
  65: export type SubmitPanOcrResponse = z.infer<typeof SubmitPanOcrResponseSchema>;
  66: export type GetKycStatusResponse = z.infer<typeof GetKycStatusResponseSchema>;
--- api/src/services/dataExport.service.ts
   1: import { userDataExportReads } from '../cosmos/user-data-export-reads.js';
   2: import { decryptPan } from './piiCrypto.service.js';
   3: 
   4: /** Bumped any time the shape of the export response changes. */
   5: export const DATA_INVENTORY_VERSION = 2; // v2: added kyc.panDecrypted (DPDP Ã‚Â§11, W2-6)
   6: 
   7: /** Audit log lookback for the export response (last 90 days). */
   8: const AUDIT_LOG_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 90;
   9: 
  10: export interface UserDataExportResponse {
  11:   dataInventoryVersion: number;
  12:   userId: string;
  13:   role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN';
  14:   /** Profile fields (technician = stored profile; customer = uid stub). */
  15:   profile: Record<string, unknown>;
  16:   bookings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  17:   ratings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  18:   complaints: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  19:   /** Technician-only Ã¢â‚¬â€ Aadhaar masked, PAN masked + decrypted (if encrypted blob present). null for customers. */
  20:   kyc: {
  21:     aadhaarMaskedNumber: string | null;
  22:     panNumber: string | null;
  23:     panDecrypted: string | null;
  24:   } | null;
  25:   /** Technician-only Ã¢â‚¬â€ financial ledger entries (retained 7y per RBI). */
  26:   walletLedger: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  27:   /** Acknowledgment of FCM topic subscriptions; never the raw token. */
  28:   fcmTokens: { acknowledged: boolean };
  29:   /** Audit log entries where this user is the resource (last 90d). */
  30:   auditLogEntries: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  31:   generatedAt: string;
  32: }
  33: 
  34: function maskPan(pan: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  35:   if (!pan) return null;
  36:   // PAN format: AAAAA9999A Ã¢â‚¬â€ mask first 5
  37:   return pan.replace(/^.{5}/, 'XXXXX');
  38: }
  39: 
  40: /**
  41:  * Project a booking for the caller. The counterparty's uid is omitted under
  42:  * DPDP Ã‚Â§11 Ã¢â‚¬â€ a data principal is entitled to *their* personal data, not the
  43:  * other party's identifier returned in machine-readable bulk form.
  44:  */
  45: function projectBooking(
  46:   b: Record<string, unknown>,
  47:   callerRole: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
  48: ): Record<string, unknown> {
  49:   return {
  50:     id: b['id'],
  51:     serviceId: b['serviceId'],
  52:     categoryId: b['categoryId'],
  53:     slotDate: b['slotDate'],
  54:     slotWindow: b['slotWindow'],
  55:     addressText: b['addressText'],
  56:     addressLatLng: b['addressLatLng'],
  57:     status: b['status'],
  58:     amount: b['amount'],
  59:     finalAmount: b['finalAmount'],
  60:     ...(callerRole === 'CUSTOMER' ? { customerId: b['customerId'] } : {}),
  61:     ...(callerRole === 'TECHNICIAN' ? { technicianId: b['technicianId'] } : {}),
  62:     createdAt: b['createdAt'],
  63:     completedAt: b['completedAt'],
  64:   };
  65: }
  66: 
  67: /**
  68:  * Project a rating for the caller. We strip the side that belongs to the
  69:  * other party Ã¢â‚¬â€ a customer's export shouldn't include the technician's
  70:  * private comment about them, and vice versa.
  71:  */
  72: function projectRating(
  73:   r: Record<string, unknown>,
  74:   callerRole: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
  75: ): Record<string, unknown> {
  76:   if (callerRole === 'CUSTOMER') {
  77:     return {
  78:       id: r['id'],
  79:       bookingId: r['bookingId'],
  80:       customerOverall: r['customerOverall'],
  81:       customerSubScores: r['customerSubScores'],
  82:       customerComment: r['customerComment'],
  83:       customerSubmittedAt: r['customerSubmittedAt'],
  84:     };
  85:   }
  86:   return {
  87:     id: r['id'],
  88:     bookingId: r['bookingId'],
  89:     techOverall: r['techOverall'],
  90:     techSubScores: r['techSubScores'],
  91:     techComment: r['techComment'],
  92:     techSubmittedAt: r['techSubmittedAt'],
  93:   };
  94: }
  95: 
  96: /**
  97:  * Project a complaint. Free-text fields (description, photoStoragePath) are
  98:  * the *filer's* personal narrative Ã¢â‚¬â€ returning them to the non-filer leaks
  99:  * the other party's testimony about them. We only emit those fields when
 100:  * the caller's role matches `filedBy`.
 101:  */
 102: function projectComplaint(
 103:   c: Record<string, unknown>,
 104:   callerRole: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
 105: ): Record<string, unknown> {
 106:   const callerIsAuthor = c['filedBy'] === callerRole;
 107:   return {
 108:     id: c['id'],
 109:     orderId: c['orderId'],
 110:     status: c['status'],
 111:     filedBy: c['filedBy'],
 112:     reasonCode: c['reasonCode'],
 113:     createdAt: c['createdAt'],
 114:     resolvedAt: c['resolvedAt'],
 115:     ...(callerIsAuthor && {
 116:       description: c['description'],
 117:       photoStoragePath: c['photoStoragePath'],
 118:     }),
 119:   };
 120: }
 121: 
 122: export async function assembleUserDataExport(
 123:   userId: string,
 124:   role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
 125: ): Promise<UserDataExportResponse> {
 126:   const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
 127:   const sinceIso = new Date(
 128:     Date.now() - AUDIT_LOG_LOOKBACK_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
 129:   ).toISOString();
 130: 
 131:   const [bookings, ratings, complaints, auditEntries] = await Promise.all([
 132:     userDataExportReads.listBookingsForUser(userId, role),
 133:     userDataExportReads.listRatingsForUser(userId, role),
 134:     userDataExportReads.listComplaintsForUser(userId),
 135:     userDataExportReads.listAuditLogForUser(userId, sinceIso),
 136:   ]);
 137: 
 138:   let profile: Record<string, unknown> = { uid: userId };
 139:   let kyc: UserDataExportResponse['kyc'] = null;
 140:   let walletLedger: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
 141:   let fcmAcknowledged = false;
 142: 
 143:   if (role === 'TECHNICIAN') {
 144:     const techDoc = await userDataExportReads.readTechnicianFullDoc(userId);
 145:     if (techDoc.profile) {
 146:       profile = { ...techDoc.profile, uid: userId };
 147:     }
 148:     if (techDoc.kyc) {
 149:       let panDecrypted: string | null = null;
 150:       if (techDoc.kyc.panNumberEncrypted) {
 151:         try {
 152:           panDecrypted = decryptPan(techDoc.kyc.panNumberEncrypted);
 153:         } catch {
 154:           // Key rotation or blob corruption Ã¢â‚¬â€ fall back to masked only
 155:           panDecrypted = null;
 156:         }
 157:       }
 158:       kyc = {
 159:         aadhaarMaskedNumber: techDoc.kyc.aadhaarMaskedNumber ?? null,
 160:         // E19-S01 migration window: prefer new panMaskedNumber, fall back to masking legacy panNumber
 161:         panNumber: techDoc.kyc.panMaskedNumber ?? maskPan(techDoc.kyc.panNumber),
 162:         panDecrypted,
 163:       };
 164:     } else {
 165:       kyc = { aadhaarMaskedNumber: null, panNumber: null, panDecrypted: null };
 166:     }
 167:     fcmAcknowledged = techDoc.fcmToken !== null && techDoc.fcmToken !== undefined;
 168:     const ledger = await userDataExportReads.listWalletLedgerForTechnician(userId);
 169:     walletLedger = ledger.map((l) => ({
 170:       id: l.id,
 171:       bookingId: l.bookingId,
 172:       bookingAmount: l.bookingAmount,
 173:       commissionAmount: l.commissionAmount,
 174:       techAmount: l.techAmount,
 175:       payoutStatus: l.payoutStatus,
 176:       createdAt: l.createdAt,
 177:       settledAt: l.settledAt,
 178:     }));
 179:   }
 180: 
 181:   return {
 182:     dataInventoryVersion: DATA_INVENTORY_VERSION,
 183:     userId,
 184:     role,
 185:     profile,
 186:     bookings: bookings.map((b) => projectBooking(b, role)),
 187:     ratings: ratings.map((r) => projectRating(r, role)),
 188:     complaints: complaints.map((c) => projectComplaint(c, role)),
 189:     kyc,
 190:     walletLedger,
 191:     fcmTokens: { acknowledged: fcmAcknowledged },
 192:     auditLogEntries: auditEntries.map((e) => ({
 193:       id: e.id,
 194:       action: e.action,
 195:       resourceType: e.resourceType,
 196:       timestamp: e.timestamp,
 197:     })),
 198:     generatedAt,
 199:   };
 200: }
--- api/src/services/formRecognizer.service.ts
   1: import { DocumentAnalysisClient, AzureKeyCredential } from '@azure/ai-form-recognizer';
   2: import { getStorageDownloadUrl } from '../firebase/admin.js';
   3: import { maskPan, hashPan } from './pan.utils.js';
   4: 
   5: type OcrResult =
   6:   | { status: 'PAN_DONE'; panMaskedNumber: string; panHash: string }
   7:   | { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW'; panMaskedNumber: null; panHash: null };
   8: 
   9: export async function extractPanFromStoragePath(
  10:   firebaseStoragePath: string
  11: ): Promise<OcrResult> {
  12:   const endpoint = process.env['FORM_RECOGNIZER_ENDPOINT'];
  13:   const key = process.env['FORM_RECOGNIZER_KEY'];
  14:   if (!endpoint || !key) {
  15:     throw new Error('FORM_RECOGNIZER_ENDPOINT and FORM_RECOGNIZER_KEY must be set');
  16:   }
  17: 
  18:   const downloadUrl = await getStorageDownloadUrl(firebaseStoragePath);
  19:   const client = new DocumentAnalysisClient(endpoint, new AzureKeyCredential(key));
  20: 
  21:   try {
  22:     const poller = await client.beginAnalyzeDocumentFromUrl('prebuilt-idDocument', downloadUrl);
  23:     const result = await poller.pollUntilDone();
  24:     const docNumber = result.documents?.[0]?.fields?.['DocumentNumber']?.content;
  25:     if (docNumber) {
  26:       const panMaskedNumber = maskPan(docNumber);
  27:       if (!panMaskedNumber) {
  28:         // Non-canonical format Ã¢â‚¬â€ route to manual review; raw discarded
  29:         return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null, panHash: null };
  30:       }
  31:       const panHash = hashPan(docNumber);
  32:       return { status: 'PAN_DONE', panMaskedNumber, panHash };
  33:     }
  34:     return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null, panHash: null };
  35:   } catch (err: unknown) {
  36:     const statusCode = (err as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode;
  37:     if (statusCode === 429) {
  38:       return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null, panHash: null };
  39:     }
  40:     throw err;
  41:   }
  42: }
--- api/src/services/pan.utils.ts
   1: import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
   2: 
   3: const PAN_REGEX = /^[A-Z]{5}\d{4}[A-Z]$/;
   4: 
   5: export function normalizePan(pan: string): string {
   6:   return pan.trim().toUpperCase();
   7: }
   8: 
   9: export function maskPan(pan: string): string | null {
  10:   const normalized = normalizePan(pan);
  11:   if (!PAN_REGEX.test(normalized)) return null;
  12:   return `XXXXX${normalized.slice(5)}`;
  13: }
  14: 
  15: export function hashPan(pan: string): string {
  16:   return createHash('sha256').update(normalizePan(pan)).digest('hex');
  17: }
--- api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts
   1: #!/usr/bin/env tsx
   2: /**
   3:  * E19-S01 migration: backfill panMaskedNumber + panHash for all technician docs
   4:  * that still have a legacy panNumber or panNumberEncrypted field.
   5:  *
   6:  * Usage:
   7:  *   pnpm tsx api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts --dry-run   # preview only
   8:  *   pnpm tsx api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts             # live run
   9:  *
  10:  * Idempotent: docs that already have panHash and no panNumber are skipped.
  11:  * Safe to run multiple times.
  12:  *
  13:  * Prerequisites:
  14:  *   - COSMOS_CONNECTION_STRING (or endpoint+key) env vars set
  15:  *   - COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY set (needed to decrypt legacy panNumberEncrypted blobs)
  16:  *   - COSMOS_DATABASE set
  17:  */
  18: 
  19: import { getCosmosClient, DB_NAME } from '../src/cosmos/client.js';
  20: import { decryptPan } from '../src/services/piiCrypto.service.js';
  21: import { maskPan, hashPan } from '../src/services/pan.utils.js';
  22: import type { EncryptedPan } from '../src/schemas/kyc.js';
  23: 
  24: const DRY_RUN = process.argv.includes('--dry-run');
  25: 
  26: interface KycSubdoc {
  27:   panNumber?: string | null;
  28:   panMaskedNumber?: string | null;
  29:   panHash?: string | null;
  30:   panNumberEncrypted?: EncryptedPan;
  31:   [key: string]: unknown;
  32: }
  33: 
  34: interface TechnicianDoc {
  35:   id: string;
  36:   kyc?: KycSubdoc;
  37:   [key: string]: unknown;
  38: }
  39: 
  40: function derivePanFields(kyc: KycSubdoc): {
  41:   panMaskedNumber: string | null;
  42:   panHash: string | null;
  43:   source: string;
  44: } {
  45:   // Strategy 1: decrypt the AES-GCM blob Ã¢â€ â€™ get raw PAN Ã¢â€ â€™ hash + mask
  46:   if (kyc.panNumberEncrypted) {
  47:     try {
  48:       const raw = decryptPan(kyc.panNumberEncrypted);
  49:       const masked = maskPan(raw);
  50:       if (masked) {
  51:         return {
  52:           panMaskedNumber: masked,
  53:           panHash: hashPan(raw),
  54:           source: 'decrypted',
  55:         };
  56:       }
  57:     } catch {
  58:       // Blob corrupt or key rotation Ã¢â‚¬â€ fall through to next strategy
  59:     }
  60:   }
  61: 
  62:   // Strategy 2: panNumber contains a raw canonical PAN (pre-masking era docs)
  63:   if (kyc.panNumber) {
  64:     const masked = maskPan(kyc.panNumber);
  65:     if (masked) {
  66:       return {
  67:         panMaskedNumber: masked,
  68:         panHash: hashPan(kyc.panNumber),
  69:         source: 'raw-pan-number',
  70:       };
  71:     }
  72: 
  73:     // Strategy 3: panNumber looks like a known-masked format Ã¢â‚¬â€ copy only if recognised.
  74:     // Only accept: old-style ABCDE####F  OR  new-style XXXXX1234F
  75:     // Anything else (OCR artifact, raw PAN with spaces, truncated) must NOT be copied
  76:     // verbatim because callers trust panMaskedNumber to be safe.
  77:     const KNOWN_MASKED = /^(?:[A-Z]{5}#{4}[A-Z]|XXXXX\d{4}[A-Z])$/;
  78:     if (KNOWN_MASKED.test(kyc.panNumber)) {
  79:       return {
  80:         panMaskedNumber: kyc.panNumber,
  81:         panHash: null,
  82:         source: 'pre-masked-no-hash',
  83:       };
  84:     }
  85: 
  86:     // Unrecognised format Ã¢â‚¬â€ leave masked null; keep encrypted blob for future retry
  87:     return { panMaskedNumber: kyc.panMaskedNumber ?? null, panHash: null, source: 'unrecognized-format' };
  88:   }
  89: 
  90:   // Preserve any mask written on a previous run so retries are idempotent
  91:   return { panMaskedNumber: kyc.panMaskedNumber ?? null, panHash: null, source: 'no-pan-data' };
  92: }
  93: 
  94: async function run(): Promise<void> {
  95:   console.log(`\nE19-S01 PAN migration (${DRY_RUN ? 'DRY RUN Ã¢â‚¬â€ no writes' : 'LIVE RUN'})\n`);
  96: 
  97:   const container = getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container('technicians');
  98: 
  99:   const { resources: docs } = await container.items
 100:     .query<TechnicianDoc>('SELECT * FROM c')
 101:     .fetchAll();
 102: 
 103:   console.log(`Total technician docs fetched: ${docs.length}\n`);
 104: 
 105:   let skipped = 0;
 106:   let processed = 0;
 107:   let noAction = 0;
 108:   let errors = 0;
 109: 
 110:   for (const doc of docs) {
 111:     const kyc = doc.kyc;
 112: 
 113:     if (!kyc) {
 114:       skipped++;
 115:       continue;
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     // Idempotent: already migrated
 119:     if (kyc.panHash && !kyc.panNumber) {
 120:       skipped++;
 121:       continue;
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     // Nothing to migrate
 125:     if (!kyc.panNumber && !kyc.panNumberEncrypted) {
 126:       noAction++;
 127:       continue;
 128:     }
 129: 
 130:     try {
 131:       const { panMaskedNumber, panHash, source } = derivePanFields(kyc);
 132: 
 133:       console.log(
 134:         `[${doc.id}] source=${source} hasMasked=${panMaskedNumber !== null} hasHash=${panHash !== null}`,
 135:       );
 136: 
 137:       if (!DRY_RUN) {
 138:         const updatedKyc: KycSubdoc = {
 139:           ...kyc,
 140:           panMaskedNumber,
 141:           panHash,
 142:           panNumber: null,
 143:         };
 144:         // Only remove the encrypted blob once a deterministic hash has been derived.
 145:         // If panHash is null the blob may still be decryptable on a future retry
 146:         // (e.g. after key rotation is corrected) Ã¢â‚¬â€ keep it to avoid irrecoverable loss.
 147:         if (panHash !== null) {
 148:           delete updatedKyc['panNumberEncrypted'];
 149:         }
 150: 
 151:         await container.items.upsert({ ...doc, kyc: updatedKyc });
 152:       }
 153: 
 154:       processed++;
 155:     } catch (err) {
 156:       console.error(`[${doc.id}] ERROR:`, err);
 157:       errors++;
 158:     }
 159:   }
 160: 
 161:   console.log(
 162:     `\nSummary: processed=${processed} skipped(already-done)=${skipped} no-action=${noAction} errors=${errors}`,
 163:   );
 164:   if (DRY_RUN) {

   1: import { app, HttpRequest, HttpResponseInit, InvocationContext } from '@azure/functions';
   2: import { extractPanFromStoragePath } from '../../services/formRecognizer.service.js';
   3: import { upsertKycStatus } from '../../cosmos/technician-repository.js';
   4: import { verifyTechnicianToken } from '../../middleware/verifyTechnicianToken.js';
   5: import { SubmitPanOcrRequestSchema } from '../../schemas/kyc.js';
   6: import { kycAuditEntry } from '../../services/kycAudit.service.js';
   7: 
   8: export async function submitPanOcr(
   9:   req: HttpRequest,
  10:   _ctx: InvocationContext
  11: ): Promise<HttpResponseInit> {
  12:   let decodedToken: { uid: string };
  13:   try {
  14:     decodedToken = await verifyTechnicianToken(req);
  15:   } catch {
  16:     return { status: 401, jsonBody: { error: 'Unauthorized' } };
  17:   }
  18: 
  19:   let body: unknown;
  20:   try {
  21:     body = await req.json();
  22:   } catch {
  23:     return { status: 400, jsonBody: { error: 'Invalid JSON' } };
  24:   }
  25: 
  26:   const parsed = SubmitPanOcrRequestSchema.safeParse(body);
  27:   if (!parsed.success) {
  28:     return { status: 422, jsonBody: { error: parsed.error.flatten() } };
  29:   }
  30: 
  31:   const { technicianId, firebaseStoragePath } = parsed.data;
  32: 
  33:   // P1-C: caller may only update their own KYC record
  34:   if (decodedToken.uid !== technicianId) {
  35:     return { status: 403, jsonBody: { error: 'Forbidden' } };
  36:   }
  37: 
  38:   const ocrResult = await extractPanFromStoragePath(firebaseStoragePath);
  39: 
  40:   if (ocrResult.status === 'PAN_DONE') {
  41:     // Raw PAN discarded inside formRecognizer.service; only hash+mask reach here
  42:     await upsertKycStatus(technicianId, {
  43:       panMaskedNumber: ocrResult.panMaskedNumber,
  44:       panHash: ocrResult.panHash,
  45:       panNumber: null,           // explicitly clear legacy field
  46:       panNumberEncrypted: undefined, // explicitly clear
  47:       panImagePath: firebaseStoragePath,
  48:       kycStatus: 'PAN_DONE',
  49:     });
  50:     void kycAuditEntry(technicianId, 'PAN', 'VERIFIED');
  51:     return {
  52:       status: 200,
  53:       jsonBody: {
  54:         kycStatus: 'PAN_DONE',
  55:         panMaskedNumber: ocrResult.panMaskedNumber,
  56:         panNumber: ocrResult.panMaskedNumber, // legacy alias Ã¢â‚¬â€ technician-app reads panNumber (migration window)
  57:       },
  58:     };
  59:   }
  60: 
  61:   // Explicitly clear any stale PAN fields Ã¢â‚¬â€ a rejected submission must not leave
  62:   // a previous panMaskedNumber visible via getKycStatus while status is MANUAL_REVIEW.
  63:   await upsertKycStatus(technicianId, {
  64:     panMaskedNumber: null,
  65:     panHash: null,
  66:     panNumber: null,
  67:     panNumberEncrypted: undefined,
  68:     panImagePath: firebaseStoragePath,
  69:     kycStatus: 'MANUAL_REVIEW',
  70:   });
  71:   void kycAuditEntry(technicianId, 'PAN', 'REJECTED');
  72:   return { status: 200, jsonBody: { kycStatus: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null } };
  73: }
  74: 
  75: app.http('submitPanOcr', {
  76:   methods: ['POST'],
  77:   authLevel: 'anonymous',
  78:   route: 'v1/kyc/pan-ocr',

import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';

const ALGO = 'aes-256-gcm';

/**
 * Mask a PAN for storage and display. Returns null if the format is not
 * canonical (AAAAA9999A). Callers must treat null as unmasked/unsafe and
 * route to MANUAL_REVIEW rather than storing the raw value.
 */
export function maskPan(pan: string): string | null {
  const match = pan.match(/^([A-Za-z]{5})(\d{4})([A-Za-z])$/);
  if (!match) return null;
  return `${match[1]}####${match[3]}`;
}

export interface EncryptedPan {
  iv: string;
  ciphertext: string;
  tag: string;
  v: 1;
}

function getKey(): Buffer {
  const b64 = process.env['COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY'];
  if (!b64) throw new Error('COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY env var not set');
  const key = Buffer.from(b64, 'base64');
  if (key.length !== 32) throw new Error('COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY must be 32 bytes');
  return key;
}

export function encryptPan(plaintext: string): EncryptedPan {
  const key = getKey();
  const iv = randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = createCipheriv(ALGO, key, iv, { authTagLength: 16 });
  const ciphertext = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, 'utf8'), cipher.final()]);
  return {
    iv: iv.toString('base64'),
    ciphertext: ciphertext.toString('base64'),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString('base64'),
    v: 1,
  };
}

export function decryptPan(blob: EncryptedPan): string {
  const key = getKey();
  const tagBuf = Buffer.from(blob.tag, 'base64');
  if (tagBuf.length !== 16) throw new Error('GCM auth tag must be exactly 16 bytes');
  const decipher = createDecipheriv(ALGO, key, Buffer.from(blob.iv, 'base64'), { authTagLength: 16 });
  decipher.setAuthTag(tagBuf);
  return Buffer.concat([
    decipher.update(Buffer.from(blob.ciphertext, 'base64')),
    decipher.final(),
  ]).toString('utf8');
}

import { userDataExportReads } from '../cosmos/user-data-export-reads.js';
import { decryptPan } from './piiCrypto.service.js';

/** Bumped any time the shape of the export response changes. */
export const DATA_INVENTORY_VERSION = 2; // v2: added kyc.panDecrypted (DPDP Â§11, W2-6)

/** Audit log lookback for the export response (last 90 days). */
const AUDIT_LOG_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 90;

export interface UserDataExportResponse {
  dataInventoryVersion: number;
  userId: string;
  role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN';
  /** Profile fields (technician = stored profile; customer = uid stub). */
  profile: Record<string, unknown>;
  bookings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  ratings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  complaints: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  /** Technician-only â€” Aadhaar masked, PAN masked + decrypted (if encrypted blob present). null for customers. */
  kyc: {
    aadhaarMaskedNumber: string | null;
    panNumber: string | null;
    panDecrypted: string | null;
  } | null;
  /** Technician-only â€” financial ledger entries (retained 7y per RBI). */
  walletLedger: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  /** Acknowledgment of FCM topic subscriptions; never the raw token. */
  fcmTokens: { acknowledged: boolean };
  /** Audit log entries where this user is the resource (last 90d). */
  auditLogEntries: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  generatedAt: string;
}

function maskPan(pan: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!pan) return null;
  // PAN format: AAAAA9999A â€” mask first 5
  return pan.replace(/^.{5}/, 'XXXXX');
}

/**
 * Project a booking for the caller. The counterparty's uid is omitted under
 * DPDP Â§11 â€” a data principal is entitled to *their* personal data, not the
 * other party's identifier returned in machine-readable bulk form.
 */
function projectBooking(
  b: Record<string, unknown>,
  callerRole: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    id: b['id'],
    serviceId: b['serviceId'],
    categoryId: b['categoryId'],
    slotDate: b['slotDate'],
    slotWindow: b['slotWindow'],
    addressText: b['addressText'],
    addressLatLng: b['addressLatLng'],
    status: b['status'],
    amount: b['amount'],
    finalAmount: b['finalAmount'],
    ...(callerRole === 'CUSTOMER' ? { customerId: b['customerId'] } : {}),
    ...(callerRole === 'TECHNICIAN' ? { technicianId: b['technicianId'] } : {}),
    createdAt: b['createdAt'],
    completedAt: b['completedAt'],
  };
}

/**
 * Project a rating for the caller. We strip the side that belongs to the
 * other party â€” a customer's export shouldn't include the technician's
 * private comment about them, and vice versa.
 */
function projectRating(
  r: Record<string, unknown>,
  callerRole: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (callerRole === 'CUSTOMER') {
    return {
      id: r['id'],
      bookingId: r['bookingId'],
      customerOverall: r['customerOverall'],
      customerSubScores: r['customerSubScores'],
      customerComment: r['customerComment'],
      customerSubmittedAt: r['customerSubmittedAt'],
    };
  }
  return {
    id: r['id'],
    bookingId: r['bookingId'],
    techOverall: r['techOverall'],
    techSubScores: r['techSubScores'],
    techComment: r['techComment'],
    techSubmittedAt: r['techSubmittedAt'],
  };
}

/**
 * Project a complaint. Free-text fields (description, photoStoragePath) are
 * the *filer's* personal narrative â€” returning them to the non-filer leaks
 * the other party's testimony about them. We only emit those fields when
 * the caller's role matches `filedBy`.
 */
function projectComplaint(
  c: Record<string, unknown>,
  callerRole: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const callerIsAuthor = c['filedBy'] === callerRole;
  return {
    id: c['id'],
    orderId: c['orderId'],
    status: c['status'],
    filedBy: c['filedBy'],
    reasonCode: c['reasonCode'],
    createdAt: c['createdAt'],
    resolvedAt: c['resolvedAt'],
    ...(callerIsAuthor && {
      description: c['description'],
      photoStoragePath: c['photoStoragePath'],
    }),
  };
}

export async function assembleUserDataExport(
  userId: string,
  role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
): Promise<UserDataExportResponse> {
  const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const sinceIso = new Date(
    Date.now() - AUDIT_LOG_LOOKBACK_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
  ).toISOString();

  const [bookings, ratings, complaints, auditEntries] = await Promise.all([
    userDataExportReads.listBookingsForUser(userId, role),
    userDataExportReads.listRatingsForUser(userId, role),
    userDataExportReads.listComplaintsForUser(userId),
    userDataExportReads.listAuditLogForUser(userId, sinceIso),
  ]);

  let profile: Record<string, unknown> = { uid: userId };
  let kyc: UserDataExportResponse['kyc'] = null;
  let walletLedger: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
  let fcmAcknowledged = false;

  if (role === 'TECHNICIAN') {
    const techDoc = await userDataExportReads.readTechnicianFullDoc(userId);
    if (techDoc.profile) {
      profile = { ...techDoc.profile, uid: userId };
    }
    if (techDoc.kyc) {
      let panDecrypted: string | null = null;
      if (techDoc.kyc.panNumberEncrypted) {
        try {
          panDecrypted = decryptPan(techDoc.kyc.panNumberEncrypted);
        } catch {
          // Key rotation or blob corruption â€” fall back to masked only
          panDecrypted = null;
        }
      }
      kyc = {
        aadhaarMaskedNumber: techDoc.kyc.aadhaarMaskedNumber ?? null,
        panNumber: maskPan(techDoc.kyc.panNumber),
        panDecrypted,
      };
    } else {
      kyc = { aadhaarMaskedNumber: null, panNumber: null, panDecrypted: null };
    }
    fcmAcknowledged = techDoc.fcmToken !== null && techDoc.fcmToken !== undefined;
    const ledger = await userDataExportReads.listWalletLedgerForTechnician(userId);
    walletLedger = ledger.map((l) => ({
      id: l.id,
      bookingId: l.bookingId,
      bookingAmount: l.bookingAmount,
      commissionAmount: l.commissionAmount,
      techAmount: l.techAmount,
      payoutStatus: l.payoutStatus,
      createdAt: l.createdAt,
      settledAt: l.settledAt,
    }));
  }

  return {
    dataInventoryVersion: DATA_INVENTORY_VERSION,
    userId,
    role,
    profile,
    bookings: bookings.map((b) => projectBooking(b, role)),
    ratings: ratings.map((r) => projectRating(r, role)),
    complaints: complaints.map((c) => projectComplaint(c, role)),
    kyc,
    walletLedger,
    fcmTokens: { acknowledged: fcmAcknowledged },

 const CONTAINER = 'technicians';
 
 // â”€â”€ KYC methods (E02-S03 pattern) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

 export async function upsertKycStatus(
   technicianId: string,
   patch: Partial<TechnicianKyc> & { kycStatus: KycStatus }
 ): Promise<void> {
   const client = getCosmosClient();
   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
   const { resource } = await container.item(technicianId, technicianId).read<TechnicianDoc>();
   const existing: TechnicianDoc = resource ?? { id: technicianId };
   const updated: TechnicianDoc = {
     ...existing,
     kyc: {
       aadhaarVerified: false,
       aadhaarMaskedNumber: null,
       panNumber: null,
+      panMaskedNumber: null,
+      panHash: null,
       panImagePath: null,
       ...(existing.kyc ?? {}),
       ...patch,
       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
     },
   };
   await container.items.upsert(updated);
 }
 
 export async function getKycByTechnicianId(
   technicianId: string
 ): Promise<TechnicianKyc | null> {
   const client = getCosmosClient();
   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
   const { resource } = await container.item(technicianId, technicianId).read<TechnicianDoc>();
   return (resource?.kyc as TechnicianKyc | undefined) ?? null;
 }
 
 // â”€â”€ Geospatial profile methods (E05-S01) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 
 export async function upsertTechnicianProfile(profile: TechnicianProfile): Promise<void> {
   const client = getCosmosClient();
   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
   await container.items.upsert(profile);
 }
 
 export interface TechnicianAvailability {
   isOnline: boolean;
   isAvailable: boolean;
   availabilityWindows: AvailabilityWindow[];
   updatedAt?: string;
 }
 
 export interface TechnicianAvailabilityPatch {
   isOnline?: boolean | undefined;
   isAvailable?: boolean | undefined;
   availabilityWindows?: AvailabilityWindow[] | undefined;
 }
 
 export interface TechnicianServiceLocation {
   lat: number;
   lng: number;
 }
 
 export interface TechnicianServiceProfile {
   skills: string[];
   location: TechnicianServiceLocation | null;
 }
 
 export interface TechnicianServiceProfilePatch {
   skills: string[];
   location?: TechnicianServiceLocation | undefined;
 }
 
 const defaultAvailabilityWindows = (): AvailabilityWindow[] =>
   Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, dayOfWeek) => [
     { dayOfWeek, startHour: 8, endHour: 12 },
     { dayOfWeek, startHour: 12, endHour: 17 },
   ]).flat();
 
 function normalizeAvailability(doc?: Partial<TechnicianProfile> & Record<string, unknown>): TechnicianAvailability {
   const availability: TechnicianAvailability = {
     isOnline: typeof doc?.isOnline === 'boolean' ? doc.isOnline : true,
     isAvailable: typeof doc?.isAvailable === 'boolean' ? doc.isAvailable : true,
     availabilityWindows: Array.isArray(doc?.availabilityWindows)
       ? doc.availabilityWindows
       : defaultAvailabilityWindows(),
   };
   if (typeof doc?.updatedAt === 'string') availability.updatedAt = doc.updatedAt;
   return availability;
 }
 
 export async function getTechnicianAvailability(technicianId: string): Promise<TechnicianAvailability> {
   const client = getCosmosClient();
   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
   const { resource } = await container.item(technicianId, technicianId).read<Partial<TechnicianProfile> & Record<string, unknown>>();
   return normalizeAvailability(resource);
 }
