 /**
  * Project a complaint. Free-text fields (description, photoStoragePath) are
  * the *filer's* personal narrative â€” returning them to the non-filer leaks
  * the other party's testimony about them. We only emit those fields when
  * the caller's role matches `filedBy`.
  */
 function projectComplaint(
   c: Record<string, unknown>,
   callerRole: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
 ): Record<string, unknown> {
   const callerIsAuthor = c['filedBy'] === callerRole;
   return {
     id: c['id'],
     orderId: c['orderId'],
     status: c['status'],
     filedBy: c['filedBy'],
     reasonCode: c['reasonCode'],
     createdAt: c['createdAt'],
     resolvedAt: c['resolvedAt'],
     ...(callerIsAuthor && {
       description: c['description'],
       photoStoragePath: c['photoStoragePath'],
     }),
   };
 }
 
 export async function assembleUserDataExport(
   userId: string,
   role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN',
 ): Promise<UserDataExportResponse> {
   const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
   const sinceIso = new Date(
     Date.now() - AUDIT_LOG_LOOKBACK_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
   ).toISOString();
 
   const [bookings, ratings, complaints, auditEntries] = await Promise.all([
     userDataExportReads.listBookingsForUser(userId, role),
     userDataExportReads.listRatingsForUser(userId, role),
     userDataExportReads.listComplaintsForUser(userId),
     userDataExportReads.listAuditLogForUser(userId, sinceIso),
   ]);
 
   let profile: Record<string, unknown> = { uid: userId };
   let kyc: UserDataExportResponse['kyc'] = null;
   let walletLedger: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
   let fcmAcknowledged = false;
 
   if (role === 'TECHNICIAN') {
     const techDoc = await userDataExportReads.readTechnicianFullDoc(userId);
     if (techDoc.profile) {
       profile = { ...techDoc.profile, uid: userId };
     }
     if (techDoc.kyc) {
       let panDecrypted: string | null = null;
       if (techDoc.kyc.panNumberEncrypted) {
         try {
           panDecrypted = decryptPan(techDoc.kyc.panNumberEncrypted);
         } catch {
           // Key rotation or blob corruption â€” fall back to masked only
           panDecrypted = null;
         }
       }
       kyc = {
         aadhaarMaskedNumber: techDoc.kyc.aadhaarMaskedNumber ?? null,
-        panNumber: maskPan(techDoc.kyc.panNumber),
+        // E19-S01 migration window: prefer new panMaskedNumber, fall back to masking legacy panNumber
+        panNumber: techDoc.kyc.panMaskedNumber ?? maskPan(techDoc.kyc.panNumber),
         panDecrypted,
       };
     } else {
       kyc = { aadhaarMaskedNumber: null, panNumber: null, panDecrypted: null };
     }
     fcmAcknowledged = techDoc.fcmToken !== null && techDoc.fcmToken !== undefined;
     const ledger = await userDataExportReads.listWalletLedgerForTechnician(userId);
     walletLedger = ledger.map((l) => ({
       id: l.id,
       bookingId: l.bookingId,
       bookingAmount: l.bookingAmount,
       commissionAmount: l.commissionAmount,
       techAmount: l.techAmount,
       payoutStatus: l.payoutStatus,
       createdAt: l.createdAt,
       settledAt: l.settledAt,
     }));
   }
 
   return {
     dataInventoryVersion: DATA_INVENTORY_VERSION,
     userId,
     role,
     profile,
     bookings: bookings.map((b) => projectBooking(b, role)),
     ratings: ratings.map((r) => projectRating(r, role)),
     complaints: complaints.map((c) => projectComplaint(c, role)),
     kyc,
     walletLedger,
     fcmTokens: { acknowledged: fcmAcknowledged },
     auditLogEntries: auditEntries.map((e) => ({
       id: e.id,
       action: e.action,
       resourceType: e.resourceType,
       timestamp: e.timestamp,
     })),
     generatedAt,
   };
 }

 export async function extractPanFromStoragePath(
   firebaseStoragePath: string
 ): Promise<OcrResult> {
   const endpoint = process.env['FORM_RECOGNIZER_ENDPOINT'];
   const key = process.env['FORM_RECOGNIZER_KEY'];
   if (!endpoint || !key) {
     throw new Error('FORM_RECOGNIZER_ENDPOINT and FORM_RECOGNIZER_KEY must be set');
   }
 
   const downloadUrl = await getStorageDownloadUrl(firebaseStoragePath);
   const client = new DocumentAnalysisClient(endpoint, new AzureKeyCredential(key));
 
   try {
     const poller = await client.beginAnalyzeDocumentFromUrl('prebuilt-idDocument', downloadUrl);
     const result = await poller.pollUntilDone();
     const docNumber = result.documents?.[0]?.fields?.['DocumentNumber']?.content;
     if (docNumber) {
-      return { status: 'PAN_DONE', panNumber: docNumber };
+      const panMaskedNumber = maskPan(docNumber);
+      if (!panMaskedNumber) {
+        // Non-canonical format â€” route to manual review; raw discarded
+        return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null, panHash: null };
+      }
+      const panHash = hashPan(docNumber);
+      return { status: 'PAN_DONE', panMaskedNumber, panHash };
     }
-    return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panNumber: null };
+    return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null, panHash: null };
   } catch (err: unknown) {
     const statusCode = (err as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode;
     if (statusCode === 429) {
-      return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panNumber: null };
+      return { status: 'MANUAL_REVIEW', panMaskedNumber: null, panHash: null };
     }
     throw err;
   }
 }

   7: import { getCosmosClient, DB_NAME, getBookingsContainer, getRatingsContainer, getWalletLedgerContainer, getDispatchAttemptsContainer, getBookingEventsContainer } from './client.js';
   8: import { BookingDocSchema } from '../schemas/booking.js';
   9: import { RatingDocSchema } from '../schemas/rating.js';
  10: import { ComplaintDocSchema } from '../schemas/complaint.js';
  11: import { WalletLedgerEntrySchema } from '../schemas/wallet-ledger.js';
  12: import { BookingEventDocSchema } from '../schemas/booking-event.js';
  13: import { DispatchAttemptDocSchema } from '../schemas/dispatch-attempt.js';
  14: import { AuditLogEntrySchema } from '../schemas/audit-log.js';
  15: import type { BookingDoc } from '../schemas/booking.js';
  16: import type { RatingDoc } from '../schemas/rating.js';
  17: import type { ComplaintDoc } from '../schemas/complaint.js';
  18: import type { WalletLedgerEntry } from '../schemas/wallet-ledger.js';
  19: import type { BookingEventDoc } from '../schemas/booking-event.js';
  20: import type { DispatchAttemptDoc } from '../schemas/dispatch-attempt.js';
  21: import type { AuditLogEntry } from '../schemas/audit-log.js';
  22: import type { TechnicianKyc } from '../schemas/kyc.js';
  23: import type { TechnicianProfile } from '../schemas/technician.js';
  24: 
  25: const COMPLAINTS_CONTAINER = 'complaints';
  26: const TECHNICIANS_CONTAINER = 'technicians';
  27: const AUDIT_LOG_CONTAINER = 'audit_log';
  28: 
  29: export interface TechnicianFullExportDoc {
  30:   profile: TechnicianProfile | null;
  31:   kyc: TechnicianKyc | null;
  32:   fcmToken: string | null;
  33: }
  34: 
  35: async function listBookingsByCustomer(uid: string): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
  36:   const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
  37:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  38:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.customerId = @uid',
  39:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  40:     })
  41:     .fetchAll();
  42:   return resources.map((r) => BookingDocSchema.parse(r));
  43: }
  44: 
  45: async function listBookingsByTechnician(uid: string): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
  46:   const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
  47:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  48:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid',
  49:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  50:     })
  51:     .fetchAll();
  52:   return resources.map((r) => BookingDocSchema.parse(r));
  53: }
  54: 
  55: async function listRatingsByCustomer(uid: string): Promise<RatingDoc[]> {
  56:   const { resources } = await getRatingsContainer()
  57:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  58:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.customerId = @uid',
  59:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  60:     })
  61:     .fetchAll();
  62:   return resources.map((r) => RatingDocSchema.parse(r));
  63: }
  64: 
  65: async function listRatingsByTechnician(uid: string): Promise<RatingDoc[]> {
  66:   const { resources } = await getRatingsContainer()
  67:     .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  68:       query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid',
  69:       parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  70:     })
  71:     .fetchAll();
  72:   return resources.map((r) => RatingDocSchema.parse(r));
  73: }
  74: 
  75: export const userDataExportReads = {
  76:   async listBookingsForUser(uid: string, role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN'): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
  77:     return role === 'CUSTOMER' ? listBookingsByCustomer(uid) : listBookingsByTechnician(uid);
  78:   },
  79: 
  80:   async listRatingsForUser(uid: string, role: 'CUSTOMER' | 'TECHNICIAN'): Promise<RatingDoc[]> {
  81:     return role === 'CUSTOMER' ? listRatingsByCustomer(uid) : listRatingsByTechnician(uid);
  82:   },
  83: 
  84:   async listComplaintsForUser(uid: string): Promise<ComplaintDoc[]> {
  85:     const { resources } = await getCosmosClient()
  86:       .database(DB_NAME)
  87:       .container(COMPLAINTS_CONTAINER)
  88:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  89:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.customerId = @uid OR c.technicianId = @uid',
  90:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
  91:       })
  92:       .fetchAll();
  93:     return resources.map((r) => ComplaintDocSchema.parse(r));
  94:   },
  95: 
  96:   async listWalletLedgerForTechnician(uid: string): Promise<WalletLedgerEntry[]> {
  97:     const { resources } = await getWalletLedgerContainer()
  98:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
  99:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid',
 100:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
 101:       })
 102:       .fetchAll();
 103:     return resources.map((r) => WalletLedgerEntrySchema.parse(r));
 104:   },
 105: 
 106:   async listBookingEventsForUser(uid: string): Promise<BookingEventDoc[]> {
 107:     const { resources } = await getBookingEventsContainer()
 108:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
 109:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.technicianId = @uid OR c.adminId = @uid',
 110:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
 111:       })
 112:       .fetchAll();
 113:     return resources.map((r) => BookingEventDocSchema.parse(r));
 114:   },
 115: 
 116:   async listDispatchAttemptsForUser(uid: string): Promise<DispatchAttemptDoc[]> {
 117:     const { resources } = await getDispatchAttemptsContainer()
 118:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
 119:         query: 'SELECT * FROM c WHERE ARRAY_CONTAINS(c.technicianIds, @uid)',
 120:         parameters: [{ name: '@uid', value: uid }],
 121:       })
 122:       .fetchAll();
 123:     return resources.map((r) => DispatchAttemptDocSchema.parse(r));
 124:   },
 125: 
 126:   async readTechnicianFullDoc(uid: string): Promise<TechnicianFullExportDoc> {
 127:     const { resource } = await getCosmosClient()
 128:       .database(DB_NAME)
 129:       .container(TECHNICIANS_CONTAINER)
 130:       .item(uid, uid)
 131:       .read<Record<string, unknown>>();
 132:     if (!resource) return { profile: null, kyc: null, fcmToken: null };
 133:     const r = resource as {
 134:       kyc?: TechnicianKyc;
 135:       fcmToken?: string;
 136:     } & Record<string, unknown>;
 137:     const { kyc, fcmToken, ...rest } = r;
 138:     return {
 139:       profile: rest as unknown as TechnicianProfile,
 140:       kyc: kyc ?? null,
 141:       fcmToken: fcmToken ?? null,
 142:     };
 143:   },
 144: 
 145:   async listAuditLogForUser(uid: string, sinceIso: string): Promise<AuditLogEntry[]> {
 146:     const { resources } = await getCosmosClient()
 147:       .database(DB_NAME)
 148:       .container(AUDIT_LOG_CONTAINER)
 149:       .items.query<Record<string, unknown>>({
 150:         query:
 151:           'SELECT * FROM c WHERE c.resourceId = @uid AND c.timestamp >= @since ORDER BY c.timestamp DESC',
 152:         parameters: [
 153:           { name: '@uid', value: uid },
 154:           { name: '@since', value: sinceIso },
 155:         ],
 156:       })
 157:       .fetchAll();
 158:     return resources.map((r) => AuditLogEntrySchema.parse(r));
 159:   },
 160: };

codex
The patch can create invalid PAN hashes from already-masked values and the migration can clobber concurrent technician document updates. These issues should be fixed before the migration or new OCR path is used.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Reject masked PANs before hashing — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\services\pan.utils.ts:3-3
  Because this regex also accepts the new masked format `XXXXX1234F`, callers that use `maskPan()` as a raw-PAN validator can treat an already-masked value as hashable raw input. For example, the migration's documented `XXXXX\d{4}[A-Z]` value takes the raw branch and stores `hashPan('XXXXX1234F')` instead of leaving `panHash` null, and OCR could likewise mark a masked value as `PAN_DONE`; reject the `XXXXX` mask or check known masks before raw hashing.

- [P2] Patch only KYC fields during migration — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\scripts\migrate-pan-to-hash.ts:151-151
  When this migration runs against a live technicians container, each item was read by the initial `SELECT *` before this upsert. If availability, profile, or other fields change after the read, this full-document upsert writes the stale `doc` copy back and loses that concurrent update; use a Cosmos patch or an ETag-guarded replace scoped to `kyc`.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Reject masked PANs before hashing — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\services\pan.utils.ts:3-3
  Because this regex also accepts the new masked format `XXXXX1234F`, callers that use `maskPan()` as a raw-PAN validator can treat an already-masked value as hashable raw input. For example, the migration's documented `XXXXX\d{4}[A-Z]` value takes the raw branch and stores `hashPan('XXXXX1234F')` instead of leaving `panHash` null, and OCR could likewise mark a masked value as `PAN_DONE`; reject the `XXXXX` mask or check known masks before raw hashing.

- [P2] Patch only KYC fields during migration — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\scripts\migrate-pan-to-hash.ts:151-151
